# Celerity: Lightning Strike
POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_3_effect = {
	#Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_lightning_strike_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_lightning_strike_3
		}
	}
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold by making an unresisted attack
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	#Add duel edge for being fast
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 3 }
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	
	
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

		}
	}
	
	if = {
	     limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		#Take a big drink
		POD_hunger_feeding_lot_from_victim_effect = { VICTIM = scope:sc_defender }
		 POD_hunger_feeding_bloodloss_effect = {
			 VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
			 COUNT = 3
			 VAMPIRE = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		 scope:sc_defender = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}
	if = {
	     limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_defender
		 }
		#Take a big drink
		POD_hunger_feeding_lot_from_victim_effect = { VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker }
		 POD_hunger_feeding_bloodloss_effect = {
			 VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
			 COUNT = 3
			 VAMPIRE = scope:sc_defender
		 }
		 scope:sc_attacker = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}

	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes

# Fortitude: Prowess from Pain
POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_prowess_from_pain_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_prowess_from_pain_3
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			# To cancel out wounded, they must have any wounded trait.
			has_trait = wounded
			# And must not have cancelled it out already.
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_2_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_3_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Otherwise, we just buff their duel edge twice.
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_feral_weapons_3
		}
	}
	
	#Add duel edge for having cool claws
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 5 }
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Oblivion: Stygian Shroud
POD_combat_move_stygian_shroud_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_stygian_shroud_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_stygian_shroud_3
		}
	}
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge by blinding opponent
	if = {
		limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_attacker
			scope:sc_defender = {
			   NOR = {
			        has_perk = oblivions_sight_perk 
				    has_trait = oblivionadvanced 
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_abyss_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
					has_trait = oculi_sight
					has_perk = goblin_face_perk
			   }
			}
		}
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 
		    this = scope:sc_defender
			scope:sc_attacker = {
			   NOR = {
			        has_perk = oblivions_sight_perk 
				    has_trait = oblivionadvanced 
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_abyss_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
					has_trait = oculi_sight
					has_perk = goblin_face_perk
			   }
			}
		}
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Fire Torch
POD_combat_move_fire_torch_3_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_torch_3
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_torch_3
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
		    }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
		    if = {
			    limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
}

# Fire Bombs
POD_combat_move_fire_bombs_3_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_bombs_3
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_bombs_3
		}
	}
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
}

# Fire Spewer
POD_combat_move_fire_spewer_3_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_spewer_3
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_fire_spewer_3
		}
	}
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			add_stress = major_stress_gain
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			add_stress = major_stress_gain
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
}

			value = flag:POD_oculi_wine_3
		}
	}
	
	if = {
	    limit = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
		remove_trait = wounded_1
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
		remove_trait = wounded_2
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
		remove_trait = wounded_3
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

# Ghost-Flame Shintai: Goblin Scorch
POD_combat_move_goblin_scorch_3_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_goblin_scorch_3
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_goblin_scorch_3
		}
	}
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	POD_chi_decrease_effect = { COUNT = 3 }
}

		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high

# Serpentis: Typhonic Maw
POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_typhonic_maw_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_typhonic_maw_3
		}
	}
	
	if = {
	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		#Steal enemies blood
		POD_hunger_feeding_sip_from_victim_effect = { VICTIM = scope:sc_defender }
		 POD_hunger_feeding_bloodloss_effect = {
		     VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
			 COUNT = 3
			 VAMPIRE = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
	}
	if = {
	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_defender		 }
		#Steal enemies blood
		POD_hunger_feeding_sip_from_victim_effect = { VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker }
		 POD_hunger_feeding_bloodloss_effect = {
		     VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
			 COUNT = 3
			 VAMPIRE = scope:sc_defender
		 }
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Daimoinon: Adramelechs Wrath
POD_combat_move_adramelechs_wrath_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_adramelechs_wrath_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_adramelechs_wrath_3
		}
	}
	
	#Add duel edge add_stress = major_stress_gain
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
	
	random_list = {
	    50 = {
		   if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		        scope:sc_defender = {
		           death = {
				       death_reason = death_duel
					   killer = scope:sc_attacker
				   }
		        }
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		        scope:sc_attacker = {
		           death = {
				       death_reason = death_duel
					   killer = scope:sc_defender
				   }
		        }
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		        scope:sc_attacker = {
		           death = {
				       death_reason = death_suicide
				   }
		        }
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		        scope:sc_defender = {
		           death = {
				       death_reason = death_suicide
				   }
		        }
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

POD_combat_move_khaibits_embrace_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_khaibits_embrace
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_khaibits_embrace
		}
	}
	
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 5 }
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	POD_hekau_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
}

# Werewolves

POD_combat_move_hamstring_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_hamstring
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_hamstring
		}
	}
	
	# Debuff enemy duel edge by biting their limbs
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
		    increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
		    increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

# DEMON

POD_combat_move_manipulate_inertia_effect = {
    if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_manipulate_inertia
		}
	}
	if = {
	    limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
		    name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_manipulate_inertia
		}
	}
	
	#Increase wound treshold
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
		    increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	POD_resolve_decrease_multiple_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
}

}
